1. Generate candidate sequences of varying lengths using analytic/structured initial guesses:
   - Power law p_i ∝ (i+1)^-1/2, maybe reverse (right singular) by symmetry? We can also mix with reverse: p_i = a * left_power + (1-a) * right_power? But the objective not symmetric under reversal? `evaluate_p(p, N, n, beta)`:
```
A = rfft(p,N)
B = irfft(A*A,N)[:2*n-1]
B = np.maximum(B,0)
sB = B.sum() # should 1
# maybe renormalize p? Sum p exactly 1; B sum ~1. Could if sB !=1 due numerical, scale B? no.
D = n*B
Dmax = D.max()
x = beta*(D-Dmax)
w = exp(x - max(x)) # max 0
s=sum(w); if s=0
wn = w/s
soft = Dmax + log(s)/beta
return A,B,Dmax,soft,wn
```
Then `gradient_from_state(p,A,wn,N,n)`:
```
W = rfft(wn,N)
corr = irfft(W*np.conj(A), N).real[:n]
corr = np.where(finite, corr,0)
mean = dot(p,corr)
dir = p*(corr - mean)
# maybe subtract mean? Gradient already weighted mean zero? dot(p,dir)=0.
# normalize RMS
r = dir
rms = sqrt(mean(r*r))
if rms small: return zero
r = r/rms
r = np.clip(r, -5,5)
return r
```


Let's design `optimize_sequence` in detail.

Inputs: p start normalized, n, seconds, seed, maybe beta_start/end, lr.
Steps:
1. v = log(p) after replacing zeros with tiny. To avoid log zero, use p = max(p, 1e-300). v=log(p); v-=max(v).
2. N=next_fast_len(2*n-1). maybe use `1 << (2*n-2).bit_length()`.
3. Evaluate initial state: A,B,Dmax,soft,w, score=2*Dmax.
4. best_p = p.copy(); best_score=score. top candidates local list keep 16? Could just keep best. We can store a few diverse candidates in global candidates with their n and p; final exact. But optimize returns best plus maybe intermediate.
5. beta = beta_start; lr=lr_start; stall=0; iter=0; start=time.
6. While time < deadline:
   a. Compute gradient direction:
      - If not finite B, reset.
      - Use current weights w from state? We can recompute weights each iteration because B changes. We have w already from previous eval of current p? After accept, we can keep w. For initial, computed. For every current p, we need weights based on its B and beta. We can store w.
      - `corr=corr_from_weights(p,A,w,N,n)`.
      - `mean=np.dot(p,corr)` (weighted mean; should be real).
      - `raw=p*(corr-mean)`.
      - If variance too small, perturb.
      - `rms=sqrt(mean(raw*raw))`; if zero reset direction.
      - `dir=raw/rms`; clip.
   b. Update p with line search:
      - `lr_try=lr`
      - for attempt in 0..5:
         v_new = v - lr_try*dir
         v_new -= max(v_new); v_new = clip? maybe not clip; but for stability if max-min > 100, rescale? We can use `v_new = np.where(finite, v_new,0); v_new -= max`.
         p_new = softmax(v_new)
         A_new, B_new, Dmax_new, soft_new, w_new = evaluate(p_new)
         score_new=2*Dmax_new
         if score_new < best_score: update best.
         Accept if soft_new <= soft + 1e-12 or score_new < score + (score so maybe)? Use metric `metric = soft_new` because soft approx. Accept if soft_new < soft or (at high beta, score_new < score and soft_new < soft*1.02). Let's be conservative: accept if soft_new <= soft + 1e-10. If reject, lr_try*=0.5.
      - After attempts, if accepted: v,p,A,B,Dmax,soft,w = new; score=score_new; if soft decreased or score decreased: stall=0 else stall++.
        If lr_try used < lr maybe? Learning adapt: if accepted at first, lr=min(0.5, lr*1.02); if accepted after halving, lr=max(min, lr*0.9); if none, lr*=0.7, stall++;
   c. Every `anneal_every` (e.g. 50) iterations: beta = min(beta_max, beta * (1 + beta_inc) or beta +). But if increase beta without adjusting, soft may jump. We can set beta according to a schedule based on iterations: `beta = beta_start * (1 + iter/decay)^1` or list. Use sequence of beta phases: each phase run a target seconds. Simpler: maintain beta, every 100 iters beta = min(beta_max, beta*1.1+1). This gradually.
      Also sometimes reduce beta if high stall to smooth: if stall>100: beta = max(beta_start, beta*0.7); lr=lr_start*0.8; perturb.
   d. Every `perturb_every` (e.g. 500) iterations or stall>200: add small Gaussian noise to v, re-evaluate, accept if not too worse or reset to best. Perturb magnitude decays? Use schedule: sig = 0.05 + 0.05*sin? We'll do if stall>150: v = v_best + noise sigma, p=softmax, evaluate; if finite accept; reset beta maybe.
   e. Time check.

Need avoid too high computation. Use `time.monotonic()`.

Detailed helper code:

```
def next_fast_len(m):
    if m<=1: return 1
    return 1 << (m-1).bit_length()
```

`safe_softmax(v)`:
```
v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
v = np.where(np.isfinite(v), v, 0.0)
if v.size == 0: return np.ones(1)
mx = float(np.max(v))
if not finite: v=np.zeros_like(v); mx=0
# To avoid overflow, if mx - min > 700, rescale? Actually subtract mx. v2=v-mx.
v2 = v - mx
v2 = np.clip(v2, -745, 0) # since mx max, no positive
e=np.exp(v2)
s=e.sum()
if s<=0: e=np.ones_like(e); s=v.size
return e/s
```
If v has large range >745, bottom exp 0; okay. But if all exp underflow? max exp 1, sum≥1.

`log_weights_from_p(p)`:

```
p=np.asarray...; p=np.maximum(p, np.finfo(float).tiny)
v=np.log(p)
v-=np.max(v)
return v
```

`evaluate_p(p, N, n, beta)`:
```

B = np.fft.irfft(A*A, N)
B = B[:2*n-1].copy()
# Due to numerical, sum B maybe not exactly 1. We can renormalize p? If sum p 1, sum B =1 within error. To maintain statistics, if sumB>0: B = B/sumB? But gradient of B sum not constant if we rescale? For objective, p sum should be 1. If numerical sumB differs 1+1e-15, no. Do not rescale B because gradient of max of actual B; small error. Could clamp negative to 0.
B = np.maximum(B, 0.0)
# Also ensure nonnegative p? p already.
Dmax = float(np.max(B)) * n
if not finite: return...
# weights:
x = beta * (n*B - Dmax)  # could be large negative to 0
# clip x for exp: x = np.clip(x, -745, 0)
e = np.exp(np.clip(x, -745, 0.0))
s = e.sum()
if s <=0:
   e = np.zeros_like(B); e[int(np.argmax(B))]=1; s=1
w = e/s
soft = Dmax + np.log(s)/beta
score = 2.0*Dmax
# Maybe include exact max if beta large? score=2Dmax
return A, B, Dmax, soft, w, score
```
Using Dmax = maxB*n. If beta=0? We won't. If beta large, x range 0 to -beta*n*delta. Clip avoids underflow.

But note: If B sum not exactly 1, Dmax*n scoring uses p sum maybe 1. p softmax sum exactly 1 to machine. Good.

Gradient corr:
```
def corr_weights(A, w, N, n):
    W = np.fft.rfft(w, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(W * np.conjugate(A), N)
    c = c[:n]
    c = np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
    return c
```
Need avoid huge arrays? okay.

Direction:
```
def make_direction(p, corr):
    corr = np.asarray(corr)
    # remove mean weighted by p? We want gradient wrt log weights: p*(corr - dot)
    dot = float(np.dot(p, corr))
    raw = p * (corr - dot)
    raw = np.where(np.isfinite(raw), raw, 0.0)
    # Optionally subtract mean raw to reduce softmax constant? weighted mean already zero. arithmetic mean not; no matter.
    ss = float(np.dot(raw, raw))
    rms = np.sqrt(ss)
    if not finite or rms < 1e-300:
        # collective? return zeros
    raw = raw / rms
    raw = np.clip(raw, -10.0, 10.0)
    return raw
```
But raw RMS normalized; after clip, RMS not 1. Could renormalize after clip. Do:
```
raw = np.clip(raw, -5, 5)
rms2 = sqrt(dot); if >0 raw/=rms2
```
Clip rarely.

```
mom = mom*momentum + (1-momentum)*dir
mom_rms = rms(mom)
if mom_rms>0: direction = mom/mom_rms else dir
```

```
def try_step(v, dir, lr):

    # Keep scale: v_new -= max(v_new)
    # If range too large? v_new = np.where(np.isfinite(v_new), v_new, 0)

```

```
def optimize_sequence(p_start, time_budget, seed=0):
    p=normalize_nonneg(p_start)
    n=p.size
    if not 512 <= n <= 12000: n=4096 p=power.
    N=next_fast_len(2*n-1)
    phases = ... filter time
    start=time.monotonic(); deadline=start+time_budget
    local_candidates=[]
    state=eval...
    best_p=p.copy(); best_score=state.score; best_v=v
    add_local_candidate(local_candidates, best_score, best_p)
    beta=phases[0].beta; lr=0.12; momentum=np.zeros(n); stall=0; iter=0
    phase_idx=0; phase_deadline=start+frac*total
    while time < deadline:
       if phase_idx < len and time >= phase_deadline: phase_idx++, beta=..., lr=0.12, momentum*=0.5, phase_deadline=start+cumfrac*total
       # gradient
       corr = corr_weights(A,w,N,n)
       dir = make_direction(p,corr)
       # momentum
       momentum = 0.8*momentum + 0.2*dir
       dir = normalize(momentum) if norm>0 else dir
       # line search
       accepted=False
       lr_try=lr
       for attempt in range(6):
           p_try,v_try = softmax_step(v, dir, lr_try)
           state_try=eval(p_try,beta)
           # update global local best if state_try.score < best_score

           if state_try.soft <= soft + 1e-12 or state_try.score < score + 1e-12:
                p=p_try; v=v_try; A,B,Dmax,soft,w,score=...
                accepted=True; break

       if accepted:
           stall=0; if attempt==0: lr=min(0.35,lr*1.02) else lr=max(0.02,lr*0.9)
       else:
           lr=max(0.005, lr*0.7); stall+=1
       iter +=1
       # stochastic perturb if stall>120
       if stall > 120:
           sigma = 0.2  # maybe decays
           v_try = best_v + rng.normal(0,sigma,n)
           p_try=softmax(v_try); state_try=eval(beta)
           if finite:
               v=p=... maybe accept if score < best_score + 0.05 or rng.random()<0.1
           momentum*=0.3; stall=0; beta = min(beta_max, beta*1.1)
       # occasional pure random from power? maybe if stall>500
    return best_p,best_score,local_candidates
```

```
def make_power_profile(n, alpha=0.5, gamma=0.0):
   i=np.arange(n, dtype=float)+1.0
   p=np.power(i, -alpha)
   if gamma !=0: p *= np.power(n - np.arange(n) + 1.0, -gamma)
   p=np.maximum(p,0); s=p.sum(); if s>0 p/=s; return p
```

`evaluate_fft` can return a namedtuple? Use tuple for speed. Let's choose tuple order `(A, B, Dmax, soft, w, score)`.
```
def evaluate_fft(p, N, n, beta):
    p = np.asarray(p, float).ravel()
    A = np.fft.rfft(p, N)
    B = np.fft.irfft(A * A, N)
    B = B[:2*n-1]
    B = np.maximum(B, 0.0)
    # remove nonfinite
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(B)):
       # try clip
       B = np.where(np.isfinite(B), B, 0.0)
    mB = float(np.max(B)) if B.size else 0.0
    Dmax = n * mB
    if not np.isfinite(Dmax):
       A = ... maybe
    x = beta * (n * B - Dmax)
    x = np.clip(x, -745.0, 0.0)
    e = np.exp(x)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
       e = np.zeros_like(B)
       if B.size:
          e[int(np.argmax(B))] = 1.0
          s=1.0
    w = e / s
    if beta > 0:
       soft = Dmax + math.log(s) / beta
    else: soft = Dmax
    score = 2.0 * Dmax
    return A, B, Dmax, soft, w, score
```
Using `math.log` requires import math. `s` can be 0 if B empty. okay.
Potential performance issue: `n * B` allocates new array; we can compute `x = beta * (B - Dmax / n)`? Since Dmax = n*mB; D = nB; D-Dmax = n(B-mB). Use `mu = Dmax / n`. `x = beta * n * (B - mu)`. This allocates. Fine. Could precompute `scale = beta*n`; `x = scale*(B - mu)`. Then clip.
The weights for logsumexp of D: exp(β(D-Dmax)) = exp(β n (B - mB)). Good.

`correlation_phi`:
```
def correlation_phi(A, w, N, n):
    W = np.fft.rfft(w, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(W * np.conjugate(A), N)
    c = c[:n]
    c = np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
    return c
```
Potential alias: If `w` is a view of B? no.

`normalize_direction`:
```
def normalize_direction(x, clip=5.0):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    x = np.where(np.isfinite(x), x, 0.0)
    ss = float(np.dot(x,x))
    if not np.isfinite(ss) or ss <= 1e-300:
       return np.zeros_like(x)
    x = x / math.sqrt(ss)
    if clip > 0:
       mx = float(np.max(np.abs(x)))
       if mx > clip:
          x = np.clip(x, -clip, clip)
          ss2 = float(np.dot(x,x))
          if ss2 > 0 and np.isfinite(ss2): x/=math.sqrt(ss2)
    return x
```

`make_direction`:
```
def make_direction(p, corr):
    dot = float(np.dot(p, corr))
    raw = p * (corr - dot)
    return normalize_direction(raw, clip=10.0)
```
Need ensure corr not scaled by factor? For gradient of soft of D: weights sum 1, dir same as B. Good.

`optimize_sequence` code details:
```
def optimize_sequence(p_start, time_budget, seed=0):
    start = time.monotonic()
    budget = min(float(time_budget), 2350.0)
    if budget <= 1: return normalize_nonneg(p_start), inf, []
    p = normalize_nonneg(p_start)
    n = p.size
    if n < 256:
       p = make_power_profile(2048); n=p.size
    elif n > MAX_N_SEARCH:
       # downsample to 8192 by averaging
       target=8192
       blocks=n//target
       if blocks>1: p=np.mean(p[:target*blocks].reshape(target,blocks),axis=1)
       else: p=p[:target]
       p=normalize_nonneg(p); n=p.size
    N=next_fast_len(2*n-1)
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    phases_b=[30,100,300,900,2500]
    phases_f=[0.08,0.18,0.28,0.30,0.16]
    # normalize fractions sum maybe 1
    v=log_weights_from_p(p)
    state=evaluate_fft(p,N,n,phases_b[0])
    A,B,Dmax,soft,w,score=state
    best_p=p.copy(); best_score=score; best_v=v.copy()
    local=[]; add_candidate_list(local,best_score,best_p,8)
    momentum=np.zeros(n)
    lr=0.12
    stall=0
    iter_no=0
    phase_idx=0
    total=budget
    cum=phases_f[0]
    phase_deadline=start+total*cum
    deadline=start+total-1.0
    beta=phases_b[0]
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       if phase_idx < len(phase_b)-1 and time.monotonic() >= phase_deadline:
           phase_idx +=1
           cum += phases_f[phase_idx]
           phase_deadline = start + total*cum
           beta = phases_b[phase_idx]
           lr = 0.12 / (1.0 + 0.3*math.sqrt(beta/100.0))? maybe.
           momentum *=0.3
           # no reset, just continue
       # if state invalid reset to best
       if not np.all(np.isfinite(p)) or not np.isfinite(score):
          p=best_p.copy(); v=best_v.copy(); state=evaluate_fft(...); ...
       # direction
       corr = correlation_phi(A,w,N,n)
       dir = make_direction(p,corr)
       nz = float(np.dot(dir,dir))
       if nz < 1e-20:
          # perturb
          dir = normalize_direction(rng.normal(0,1,size=n), clip=3)
       else:
          momentum = 0.8*momentum + 0.2*dir
          mom_norm = float(np.dot(momentum,momentum))
          if mom_norm > 1e-20:
             dir = normalize_direction(momentum, clip=5)
       # line search
       accepted=False
       accepted_attempt=-1
       lr_try=lr
       for attempt in range(6):
           v_try = v - lr_try*dir
           v_try = np.where(np.isfinite(v_try), v_try, 0.0)
           v_try -= float(np.max(v_try))
           v_try = np.clip(v_try, -700.0, 0.0)
           p_try = safe_softmax(v_try)
           st_try=evaluate_fft(p_try,N,n,beta)
           A_try,B_try,Dmax_try,soft_try,w_try,score_try=st_try
           if np.isfinite(score_try):
               if score_try < best_score:
                   best_score=score_try; best_p=p_try.copy(); best_v=v_try.copy(); add_candidate_list(local,best_score,best_p,8)
               if soft_try <= soft + 1e-12 or score_try < score + 1e-12:
                   p=p_try; v=v_try; A=A_try; B=B_try; Dmax=Dmax_try; soft=soft_try; w=w_try; score=score_try
                   accepted=True; accepted_attempt=attempt; break
           lr_try*=0.5
       if accepted:
           stall=0
           if accepted_attempt ==0: lr=min(0.35, lr*1.03)
           else: lr=max(0.02, lr*0.85)
       else:
           stall +=1
           lr=max(0.005, lr*0.7)
           # if very long stall, reset current to best and try perturb
           if stall > 80:
               sigma = 0.1 + 0.2*float((stall % 100) / 100.0)
               v_try = best_v + rng.normal(0.0, sigma, size=n)
               v_try = np.where(np.isfinite(v_try), v_try, 0.0)
               v_try -= float(np.max(v_try)); v_try = np.clip(v_try, -700.0,0.0)
               p_try = safe_softmax(v_try)
               st_try=evaluate_fft(p_try,N,n,beta)
               if np.isfinite(st_try[5]):
                   p=p_try; v=v_try; A=st_try[0]; B=st_try[1]; Dmax=st_try[2]; soft=st_try[3]; w=st_try[4]; score=st_try[5]
               else:
                   p=best_p.copy(); v=best_v.copy(); A,B,Dmax,soft,w,score = evaluate_fft(p,N,n,beta)
               momentum = np.zeros(n)
               stall=0
               lr=0.12
       iter_no +=1
       # optional periodic if score close to target? no
       # To prevent time overrun, check every 100
    # final local candidate add best
    add_candidate_list(local,best_score,best_p,8)
    return best_p,best_score,local
```

```
_LBF = None
def lbfgs_objective(v, grad):
   st = _LBF
   p = safe_softmax(v)
   A = np.fft.rfft(p, st['N'])
   B = np.fft.irfft(A*A, st['N'])[:2*st['n']-1]
   B = np.maximum(B,0)
   D = st['n']*B
   Dmax = float(np.max(D))
   beta=st['beta']
   x=beta*(D-Dmax); x=np.clip(x,-745,0)
   e=np.exp(x); s=e.sum()
   val = Dmax + math.log(s)/beta
   if grad:
       w=e/s
       W=np.fft.rfft(w, st['N'])
       c=np.fft.irfft(W*np.conjugate(A), st['N']).real[:st['n']]
       dot=float(np.dot(p,c))
       g=p*(c-dot)
       # no factor beta? L = Dmax + log(s)/β. dL/dp = weights * dD? D=nB. dD/dp = n∇B; gradient of Dmax? As above LSE. Gradient = n * p*(corr - dot). Let's derive: val = (1/β)log∑e^{βD} = Dmax + log∑e^{β(D-Dmax)}/β. derivative = ∑w ∂D = n ∑w ∂B. ∑w ∂B derivative corr direction. So g = n * p*(corr-dot). In our previous direction we omitted n factor (absorbed lr). For L-BFGS, need actual gradient w.r.t v. Use g = n * p*(corr-dot).
       g = np.where(np.isfinite(g), g, 0)
       return val, g
   return val
```

`add_candidate_list`:
```
def add_candidate_list(cands, score, p, max_items=16):
    if not np.isfinite(score): return cands
    p_arr=np.asarray(p,float).ravel()
    s=p_arr.sum()
    if s<=0: return cands
    p_arr=p_arr/s
    # avoid duplicates? If len small, linear.
    for sc, arr in cands:
        if arr.size == p_arr.size and float(np.max(np.abs(arr-p_arr))) < 1e-12:
            return cands
    if len(cands) < max_items:
        cands.append((score, p_arr.copy()))
    else:
        worst=score
        idx=-1
        for i, (sc,arr) in enumerate(cands):
            if sc > worst:
                worst=sc; idx=i
        if idx>=0 and score < worst:
            cands[idx]=(score, p_arr.copy())
    return cands
```
Duplicate check O(max*n) but max small; doing every improvement maybe heavy (comparing 8192 array). Could skip duplicate check for speed; if called every step when score improves, but improvements limited. We can call not every step, only when score < best - 1e-7. In code, we call when score_try < best_score; each time improvement, candidate list maybe update. Duplicate check with up to 8 arrays okay, improvements maybe hundreds; 8*8192*100 ~6M, okay. But in inner line search, if many small improvements, could be overhead. We can only add when `score_try < best_score - 1e-8`. Use threshold.
```
if score_try < best_score - 1e-9:
   best_score=score_try; best_p=...; add_candidate_list(...)
```

`load_parent_sequence`:

```
def load_parent_sequence(max_n=MAX_N_SEARCH):
   obj = globals().get('height_sequence_1', None)
   if obj is None: return None
   try: arr=np.asarray(obj,dtype=float).ravel()
   except: return None
   if arr.size<256: return None
   arr=np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr,0)
   arr=np.maximum(arr,0)
   s=arr.sum(); if s<=0 return None
   arr/=s
   if arr.size > max_n:
      target = 8192 if max_n >=8192 else 4096
      blocks=arr.size//target
      use=target*blocks
      if blocks>1:
         arr=np.mean(arr[:use].reshape(target,blocks), axis=1)
      else:
         arr=arr[:target]
      s=arr.sum(); if s>0: arr/=s
   if arr.size > max_n: arr=arr[:max_n]; normalize
   return arr
```

```
    s=sorted(cands,key=candidate_score_key)
```

```
def finalize_sequence(p):
   p=np.asarray(p,float).ravel()
   if p.size==0: p=np.ones(64)
   p=np.where(np.isfinite(p),p,0)
   p=np.maximum(p,0)
   s=float(p.sum())
   if s<=0: p=np.ones(p.size); s=p.size
   n=p.size
   scale=math.sqrt(2.0*n)
   # normalize then scale to target sum
   p = p * (scale / s)
   p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
   s2=float(p.sum())
   if s2 < 0.01:
       p = p * (0.01 / s2); p=np.clip(p,0,1000)
   # Round? no.
   # Ensure list
   return [float(x) for x in p]
```

`make_power_profile`: if `smooth <=0`, base = i + max(0.1). Use.
```
def make_power_profile(n, alpha=0.5, smooth=1.0, right_gamma=0.0):
   i=np.arange(n,dtype=float)
   base=i+max(smooth,1e-6)
   p=np.power(base, -float(alpha))
   if abs(right_gamma)>1e-12:
      j=(n-1.0)-i+max(smooth,1e-6)
      p *= np.power(j, -float(right_gamma))
   p=np.where(np.isfinite(p),p,0)
   s=p.sum()
   if s>0: p/=s
   else: p=np.ones(n)/n
   return p
```
If alpha >0, p decreasing; if alpha=0, p=1. Good.
`make_exponential_profile`: `t = i/(max(n-1,1))`; p=exp(-lam*t) *? To include some singular? no. Normalize.

Let's write `normalize_nonneg` robust:
```
def normalize_nonneg(v):
   arr=np.asarray(v,dtype=float).ravel()
   if arr.size==0: arr=np.ones(1)
   arr=np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr,0)
   arr=np.maximum(arr,0)
   s=float(arr.sum())
   if not np.isfinite(s) or s<=0: arr=np.ones(arr.size)/max(arr.size,1)
   else: arr=arr/s
   return arr
```

`polish_with_lbfgs` code:
```
def polish_with_lbfgs(p, beta=200.0, maxiter=80):
   if _SCIPY_OPT is None: return p, inf
   n=p.size; N=next_fast_len(2*n-1)
   v=log_weights_from_p(p)
   global _LBF_STATE
   _LBF_STATE = {'N':N,'n':n,'beta':float(beta),'scale':100.0}
   try:
      res=_SCIPY_OPT.minimize(lbfgs_objective, v, jac=True, method='L-BFGS-B', options={'maxiter':maxiter,'maxls':10,'gtol':1e-8,'ftol':1e-12,'maxcor':10,'disp':False})
   except Exception:
      res=None
   if res is not None and res.x is not None:
      p_new=safe_softmax(np.asarray(res.x,float))
      st=evaluate_fft(p_new,N,n,beta)
      if st[5] < inf:
         return p_new, st[5]
   return p, inf
```

```
def fft_max_score(p):
    p=normalize_nonneg(p); n=p.size; if n==0...
    N=next_fast_len(2*n-1)
    A=np.fft.rfft(p,N)
    B=np.fft.irfft(A*A,N)[:2*n-1]
    B=np.maximum(B,0)
    mx=float(np.max(B))*n
    return 2.0*mx
```
This is same as score part of evaluate. Use initial candidate scores. For exact compare, FFT score.

```
def best_structured_start(n, beta=80.0):
    N=next_fast_len(2*n-1)
    best_score=inf; best_p=None
    options=[]
    for alpha in (0.45,0.5,0.55,0.6):
       for smooth in (0.5,1.0,2.0):
          options.append(make_power_profile(n, alpha, smooth, 0.0))
       options.append(make_power_profile(n, alpha, 1.0, 0.1))
    for lam in (0.5,1.0,2.0): options.append(make_exponential_profile(n,lam))
    for lw in (0.8,0.9): options.append(make_mixed_profile(n,lw))
    if not options: p=make_power_profile(n); ...
    for p in options:
       st=evaluate_fft(p,N,n,beta)

          best_score=st[5]; best_p=p.copy()
    if best_p is None: best_p=make_power_profile(n); best_score=fft_max_score(best_p)
    return best_p,best_score
```

Detailed optimize code:
```
def optimize_sequence(p_start, time_budget, seed=0):
    start=time.monotonic()
    budget=float(time_budget)
    if budget <=1.0:
       p=normalize_nonneg(p_start); st=evaluate_fft(p, next_fast_len(2*p.size-1), p.size, 50); return p, st[5], [(st[5], p)]
    p=normalize_nonneg(p_start)
    n=p.size
    if n < 512: p=make_power_profile(2048); n=p.size
    elif n > MAX_N_SEARCH:
       target=8192
       blocks=n//target
       if blocks>1:
          use=target*blocks
          p=np.mean(p[:use].reshape(target, blocks), axis=1)
       else:
          p=p[:target]
       p=normalize_nonneg(p); n=p.size
    N=next_fast_len(2*n-1)
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    phase_b=[20,80,250,800,2500]
    phase_f=[0.10,0.20,0.30,0.25,0.15]
    # scale fractions if budget small? okay
    v=log_weights_from_p(p)
    beta=phase_b[0]
    A,B,Dmax,soft,w,cur_score = evaluate_fft(p,N,n,beta)
    best_p=p.copy(); best_score=cur_score; best_v=v.copy()
    local=[]; add_candidate_list(local,best_score,best_p,8)
    momentum=np.zeros(n)
    lr=base_learning_rate(beta)
    stall=0; iter_no=0
    phase_idx=0
    total=budget
    cum=phase_f[0]
    phase_deadline=start+total*cum
    deadline=start+total-1.0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       if phase_idx < len(phase_b)-1 and time.monotonic() >= phase_deadline:
          phase_idx +=1
          cum += phase_f[phase_idx]
          phase_deadline = start + total*cum
          beta = phase_b[phase_idx]
          lr = base_learning_rate(beta)
          momentum *= 0.3
       if not np.isfinite(cur_score) or not np.all(np.isfinite(p)):
          p=best_p.copy(); v=best_v.copy()
          A,B,Dmax,soft,w,cur_score=evaluate_fft(p,N,n,beta)
          momentum=np.zeros(n); stall=0
          continue
       # if current much worse than best, reset
       if cur_score > best_score + 0.05:
          p=best_p.copy(); v=best_v.copy()
          A,B,Dmax,soft,w,cur_score=evaluate_fft(p,N,n,beta)
          momentum=np.zeros(n)
          continue
       corr = correlation_phi(A,w,N,n)
       d = make_direction(p, corr)
       nz=float(np.dot(d,d))
       if nz < 1e-18:
          d = normalize_direction(rng.normal(0.0,1.0,size=n), clip=3.0)
       else:
          momentum = 0.7*momentum + 0.3*d
          md = normalize_direction(momentum, clip=5.0)
          if float(np.dot(md,md)) > 1e-18:
             d = md
       accepted=False; accepted_attempt=-1
       lr_try=lr
       for attempt in range(5):
          v_try = v - lr_try * d
          v_try = np.where(np.isfinite(v_try), v_try, 0.0)
          mxv = float(np.max(v_try)) if v_try.size else 0.0
          v_try = v_try - mxv
          v_try = np.clip(v_try, -700.0, 0.0)
          p_try = safe_softmax(v_try)
          st_try = evaluate_fft(p_try,N,n,beta)
          A_try,B_try,Dmax_try,soft_try,w_try,score_try = st_try
          if np.isfinite(score_try):
             if score_try < best_score - 1e-9:
                 best_score=score_try; best_p=p_try.copy(); best_v=v_try.copy()
                 add_candidate_list(local,best_score,best_p,8)
             if score_try < cur_score - 1e-10:
                 accepted=True; accepted_attempt=attempt
                 p=p_try; v=v_try; A=A_try; B=B_try; Dmax=Dmax_try; soft=soft_try; w=w_try; cur_score=score_try
                 break
             if soft_try < soft - 1e-10 and score_try <= cur_score + 0.005:
                 accepted=True; accepted_attempt=attempt
                 p=p_try; v=v_try; A=A_try; B=B_try; Dmax=Dmax_try; soft=soft_try; w=w_try; cur_score=score_try
                 break
          lr_try *= 0.5
       if accepted:
          stall=0
          if accepted_attempt == 0:
              lr = min(0.35, lr * 1.03)
          else:
              lr = max(0.02, lr * 0.85)
       else:
          stall += 1
          lr = max(0.005, lr * 0.7)
          if stall > 80:
              sigma = 0.1 + 0.1 * (min(stall, 200) - 80) / 120.0
              v_try = best_v + rng.normal(0.0, sigma, size=n)
              v_try = np.where(np.isfinite(v_try), v_try, 0.0)
              v_try -= float(np.max(v_try))
              v_try = np.clip(v_try, -700.0, 0.0)
              p_try = safe_softmax(v_try)
              st_try = evaluate_fft(p_try,N,n,beta)
              score_try = st_try[5]
              if np.isfinite(score_try) and (score_try < best_score + 0.1 or rng.random() < 0.2):
                  p=p_try; v=v_try; A=st_try[0]; B=st_try[1]; Dmax=st_try[2]; soft=st_try[3]; w=st_try[4]; cur_score=score_try
              else:
                  p=best_p.copy(); v=best_v.copy()
                  A,B,Dmax,soft,w,cur_score=evaluate_fft(p,N,n,beta)
              momentum=np.zeros(n)
              stall=0
              lr=base_learning_rate(beta)
       iter_no +=1
       if iter_no % 1000 == 0:
          print("opt n=%d iter=%d score=%.6f best=%.6f beta=%d" % (n, iter_no, cur_score, best_score, beta))
    add_candidate_list(local,best_score,best_p,8)
    return best_p,best_score,local
```

`fft_score_only`:
```
def fft_score_only(p):
    p=normalize_nonneg(p)
    n=p.size
    if n == 0: return inf
    N=next_fast_len(2*n-1)
    A=np.fft.rfft(p,N)
    B=np.fft.irfft(A*A,N)[:2*n-1]
    if B.size ==0: return inf
    B=np.where(np.isfinite(B),B,0.0)
    if float(np.min(B))<0: B=np.maximum(B,0)
    mx=float(np.max(B))
    if not np.isfinite(mx): return inf
    return 2.0*float(n)*mx
```
For sum p=1. Good.

In `evaluate_fft`, we can avoid recomputing `n*B` by using `scale_beta = beta * n; mu = Dmax / n; x=scale_beta*(B-mu)`. Dmax = n*mx. `soft = Dmax + log(s)/beta`. If beta=0? not.

`correlation_phi` takes `w`; w length 2n-1. If w is not contiguous? rfft okay.

`add_candidate_list`: if `max_items` small. It should remove nonfinite. Use `float(score)`.

```

```

In `lbfgs_objective`, if `grad` True, return tuple; if False, return float. SciPy with `jac=True` calls with True. Good.
Set objective returns `float(val)`. If gradient, `g = scale * n * p * (corr - dot)`. Wait actual gradient wrt v = ∂val/∂p * Jacobian. ∂val/∂v = p*(∂val/∂p - dot p ∂val/∂p). ∂val/∂p = n ∑w ∂B = 2n corr? We use corr (without 2). Since constant factor 2 absorbed? For true gradient direction, if we omit factor 2, objective derivative half. L-BFGS needs exact gradient. We need include factor 2? Let's derive: L = (1/β)log∑e^{βD}, D_k=nB_k. ∂L/∂p_j = (1/β)∑ e^{βD}/s * β n ∂B_k/∂p_j = n∑w n? Wait ∂D/∂p = n∂B/∂p. ∂B/∂p = 2corr (corr as before). So ∂L/∂p = 2n (corr_w). Then ∂L/∂v_j = p_j(2n corr_j - p_j ∑p_i 2n corr_i) = 2n p_j(corr_j - dot). The factor 2 is constant for all components, so direction same but magnitude scaled. L-BFGS step size may depend on magnitude; if we omit 2, gradient half. Use 2 for correctness. Earlier `make_direction` omitted factor 2 for normalized direction; okay. But lbfgs should use 2. So `g = scale * (2.0 * n) * p*(corr-dot)`. If scale=100, for n=8192, actual grad magnitude: corr~1/n=1e-4, p dot? raw~1/n^2=1e-8, 2n=16384 =>0.00016, scale 100 =>0.016. Good. Use 2.
Also val scale same: `val = scale * soft`. Then gradient corresponds to scaled val: actual_grad_scaled = scale*actual_grad = scale*2n raw. Good.

In `optimize_sequence`, direction normalized so factor irrelevant.

In `lbfgs_objective`, compute `w = e/s`. Then `corr=correlation_phi(A,w,N,n)`. `dot=float(np.dot(p,corr))`. `g = (2.0 * n * st['scale']) * p * (corr - dot)`. Good.

Now, final `propose_candidate` code details:
```
def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    t0=time.monotonic()
    try: total=float(budget_s)
    except: total=2400.0
    total=min(max(total,0.0),2395.0)
    if total < 10:
       p=load_parent_sequence(MAX_N_SEARCH) or make_power_profile(2048)
       return finalize_sequence(p)
    cands=[]
    print("proposing with budget", total)
    final_reserve = 90.0 if total>200 else 20.0
    opt_budget = max(10.0, total - final_reserve - 5.0)
    parent=load_parent_sequence(MAX_N_SEARCH)
    starts=[]
    if parent is not None:
       sc=fft_score_only(parent)
       add_candidate_list(cands,sc,parent,32)
       print("parent score", sc)
    n8192 = 8192 if total>600 else 4096
    p8192, sc8152 = best_structured_start(n8192)
    add_candidate_list(cands, sc8152, p8192, 32)
    # choose other sizes
    raw_jobs=[]
    if parent is not None:
        raw_jobs.append((parent,0.28))
    raw_jobs.append((p8192,0.36 if parent is not None else 0.45))
    nsmall=4096 if n8192>4096 else 2048
    psmall, scsmall = best_structured_start(nsmall)
    add_candidate_list(cands, scsmall, psmall, 32)
    raw_jobs.append((psmall,0.16 if parent is not None else 0.15))
    if total > 1500:
        pbig, scbig = best_structured_start(16384)
        add_candidate_list(cands, scbig, pbig, 32)
        raw_jobs.append((pbig,0.20 if parent is not None else 0.40))
    # normalize fractions to opt_budget
    tot_frac=sum(f for _,f in raw_jobs)
    for i,(p0,f) in enumerate(raw_jobs):
       secs = opt_budget * f / tot_frac
       if secs < 20.0:
          # rerun? skip if too short
          continue
       print("job", i, "n", p0.size, "secs", secs)
       best_p, best_score, local = optimize_sequence(p0, secs, seed=seed+i*101)
       for item in local:
          add_candidate_list(cands, item[0], item[1], 48)
       if best_p is not None and np.isfinite(best_score):
          add_candidate_list(cands, best_score, best_p, 48)
       print("job done best", best_score)
       if time.monotonic() > total - final_reserve:
          break
    # L-BFGS polish top candidates
    remaining = total - 20.0 - (time.monotonic()-t0)
    if remaining > 60 and _SCIPY_OPT is not None:
        top = sorted(cands, key=candidate_score_key)[:4]
        for sc,p in top:
            rem = total - 20.0 - (time.monotonic()-t0)
            if rem < 30: break
            if p.size > MAX_N_SEARCH: continue
            print("polishing n", p.size, "fft", sc)
            pn, scn = polish_with_lbfgs(p, beta=400.0, maxiter=70)
            if np.isfinite(scn):
               add_candidate_list(cands, scn, pn, 48)
    # exact refine
    rem = total - 5.0 - (time.monotonic()-t0)
    exact_limit = min(90.0, max(0.0, rem))
    best_p_final, best_exact = refine_candidates(cands, exact_limit)
    if best_p_final is None or not np.isfinite(best_exact):
        # choose best FFT
        if cands:
            best_item = min(cands, key=candidate_score_key)  # no lambda? min key function top-level.
            # Python min key can use function name
            best_p_final = best_item[1]
            print("no exact, fft best", best_item[0])
        else:
            best_p_final = make_power_profile(4096)
    print("final candidate score", best_exact if np.isfinite(best_exact) else 'fft')
    return finalize_sequence(best_p_final)
```

`refine_candidates` implementation:
```
def refine_candidates(cands, time_limit):
   start=time.monotonic()
   if time_limit <=0 or not cands: return None, inf
   s=sorted(cands, key=candidate_score_key)
   best_score=inf; best_p=None
   for sc,p in s:
       if time.monotonic()-start > max(0.0, time_limit*0.8): break
       n=p.size
       if n > MAX_EXACT_N: continue
       if best_score < inf and sc > best_score + 1e-4:
           # Since sorted by ff score, rest worse FFT; if best exact found and current FFT clearly worse, break
           break
       sc2=exact_score_normalized(p)
       dt=time.monotonic()-start
       print("exact n", n, "fft", sc, "exact", sc2)
       if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_score:
           best_score=sc2; best_p=p.copy()
       if best_score < 1.4: break
   return best_p, best_score
```
If no exact because n>MAX, loop continues; if all n>MAX and sorted, we skip all and return None. Then final uses FFT. Good.
The break condition `sc > best_score + 1e-4` after some exact found; but if best exact from large n maybe 1.502, current sc 1.503 could exact be 1.501? FFT error unlikely >0.001. 1e-4 maybe too tight; use 5e-4. Since target small, okay. Use `5e-4`.
No exact for large candidate if skipped and best exact inf, loop continues; if all skip, no exact. Good.

Could exact the top even if n>MAX by using FFT? We skip to avoid risk. If best candidate n=16384 and MAX=18000, exact allowed. Good.

```python
"""
Search for nonnegative step-function heights minimizing

    2 * n * max(np.convolve(a, a)) / (sum(a)^2)

The program optimizes in the scale-invariant normalization sum(a)=1.
It uses FFT-based linear convolution, a softened max-of-coefficients loss in
log-probability coordinates, annealed beta (sharpening), momentum/stochastic
restarts, several structured starts (endpoint power laws, exponentials,
mixtures), optional L-BFGS-B polishing of the smooth surrogate, and final
direct np.convolve verification of the best candidates.
"""

import numpy as np
import time
import math

try:
    import scipy.optimize as _SCIPY_OPT
except Exception:
    _SCIPY_OPT = None

_LBF_STATE = None

MAX_N_SEARCH = 16384
MAX_EXACT_N = 18000


def next_fast_len(m):
    """Smallest power of two >= m."""
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def normalize_nonneg(v):
    """Return a finite nonnegative vector normalized to sum 1."""
    arr = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if arr.size == 0:
        arr = np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        arr = np.ones(max(1, arr.size), dtype=np.float64)
        s = float(arr.size)
    return arr / s


def safe_softmax(v):
    """Stable softmax to a probability vector."""
    arr = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if arr.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    mx = float(np.max(arr))
    if not np.isfinite(mx):
        arr = np.zeros_like(arr)
        mx = 0.0
    arr2 = arr - mx
    arr2 = np.clip(arr2, -700.0, 0.0)
    e = np.exp(arr2)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        e = np.ones_like(e)
        s = float(e.size)
    return e / s


def log_weights_from_p(p):
    """Log weights for a nonnegative probability vector."""
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if p.size == 0:
        p = np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
    p = np.maximum(p, np.finfo(np.float64).tiny)
    v = np.log(p)
    v = np.where(np.isfinite(v), v, 0.0)
    v -= float(np.max(v))
    return v


def make_power_profile(n, alpha=0.5, smooth=1.0, right_gamma=0.0):
    """Endpoint power-law profile, optionally with a mild right-side gamma."""
    n = int(max(1, n))
    i = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    base = i + max(float(smooth), 1e-6)
    p = np.power(base, -float(alpha))
    if abs(float(right_gamma)) > 1e-12:
        j = (n - 1.0) - i + max(float(smooth), 1e-6)
        p *= np.power(j, -float(right_gamma))
    p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
    p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        p = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    else:
        p = p / s
    return p


def make_exponential_profile(n, lam=1.0):
    """Exponential taper from one endpoint."""
    n = int(max(1, n))
    if n == 1:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    t = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n - 1)
    p = np.exp(-float(lam) * t)
    p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
    p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        p = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    else:
        p = p / s
    return p


def make_mixed_profile(n, left_weight=0.85):
    """Mixture of left and right endpoint power-law profiles."""
    left = make_power_profile(n, 0.5, 1.0, 0.0)
    right = left[::-1].copy()
    a = float(left_weight)
    if not np.isfinite(a) or a <= 0.0:
        a = 1.0
    if a > 1.0:
        a = 1.0
    p = a * left + (1.0 - a) * right
    p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
    p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        p = left.copy()
    else:
        p = p / s
    return p


def fft_score_only(p):
    """FFT-based exact-max score 2*n*max(conv) for sum(p)=1."""
    p = normalize_nonneg(p)
    n = p.size
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf")
    N = next_fast_len(2 * n - 1)
    A = np.fft.rfft(p, N)
    B = np.fft.irfft(A * A, N)[: 2 * n - 1]
    B = np.where(np.isfinite(B), B, 0.0)
    B = np.maximum(B, 0.0)
    if B.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    mx = float(np.max(B))
    if not np.isfinite(mx):
        return float("inf")
    return float(2.0 * n * mx)


def evaluate_fft(p, N, n, beta):
    """
    Evaluate a normalized nonnegative sequence using FFT.
    Returns (A, B, Dmax, soft_loss, weights, score), where score = 2*Dmax
    and Dmax = n*max(B).
    """
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if p.size == 0:
        return None, np.array([0.0]), 0.0, float("inf"), np.array([1.0]), float("inf")

    A = np.fft.rfft(p, N)
    B = np.fft.irfft(A * A, N)[: 2 * n - 1]
    B = np.where(np.isfinite(B), B, 0.0)
    B = np.maximum(B, 0.0)

    if B.size == 0:
        mx = 0.0
    else:
        mx = float(np.max(B))
    if not np.isfinite(mx):
        mx = 0.0

    Dmax = float(n) * mx
    score = float(2.0 * Dmax)

    if beta <= 0.0:
        w = np.full(B.size, 1.0) if B.size else np.array([1.0])
        if float(w.sum()) <= 0.0:
            w[0] = 1.0
        soft = Dmax
        return A, B, Dmax, soft, w, score

    # D = n*B, target max Dmax.  Weights exp(beta*(D-Dmax)), normalized.
    mu = Dmax / float(n)
    x = float(beta) * float(n) * (B - mu)
    x = np.clip(x, -745.0, 0.0)
    e = np.exp(x)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        e = np.zeros_like(B)
        if B.size:
            e[int(np.argmax(B))] = 1.0
            s = 1.0
    w = e / s
    soft = Dmax + float(math.log(s)) / float(beta)
    return A, B, Dmax, soft, w, score


def correlation_phi(A, w, N, n):
    """
    c_j = sum_i p_i * w_{i+j}, j=0..n-1, from keep relations of weights w
    to sequence coefficients stored in A = rfft(p,N).
    """
    W = np.fft.rfft(w, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(W * np.conjugate(A), N)
    c = c[:n]
    c = np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
    return c


def normalize_direction(x, clip=5.0):
    """Normalize a vector to RMS 1, with optional clip and renormalization."""
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    x = np.where(np.isfinite(x), x, 0.0)
    ss = float(np.dot(x, x))
    if not np.isfinite(ss) or ss <= 1e-300:
        return np.zeros_like(x)
    x = x / math.sqrt(ss)
    if clip > 0.0:
        mx = float(np.max(np.abs(x))) if x.size else 0.0
        if np.isfinite(mx) and mx > clip:
            x = np.clip(x, -clip, clip)
            ss2 = float(np.dot(x, x))
            if np.isfinite(ss2) and ss2 > 0.0:
                x = x / math.sqrt(ss2)
    return x


def make_direction(p, corr):
    """
    Gradient direction wrt log-probability weights for the weighted linear
    surrogate sum_k w_k B_k.  Constants are irrelevant after normalization.
    """
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    corr = np.asarray(corr, dtype=np.float64)
    corr = np.where(np.isfinite(corr), corr, 0.0)
    dot = float(np.dot(p, corr))
    raw = p * (corr - dot)
    return normalize_direction(raw, clip=10.0)


def base_learning_rate(beta):
    """Moderate learning rate depending on sharpening beta."""
    b = max(0.0, float(beta))
    return 0.16 / (1.0 + 0.25 * math.sqrt(b / 100.0))


def add_candidate_list(cands, score, p, max_items=32):
    """Append a candidate if it appears better than the current worst."""
    if not np.isfinite(score):
        return cands
    arr = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if arr.size == 0:
        return cands
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return cands
    arr = arr / s

    # Cheap duplicate filter.
    for sc2, arr2 in cands:
        if arr2.size == arr.size and abs(float(sc2) - float(score)) < 1e-12:
            return cands

    if len(cands) < max_items:
        cands.append((float(score), arr.copy()))
        return cands

    worst_idx = -1
    worst_score = float("inf")
    for i in range(len(cands)):
        if cands[i][0] > worst_score:
            worst_score = cands[i][0]
            worst_idx = i

    if worst_idx >= 0 and score < worst_score:
        cands[worst_idx] = (float(score), arr.copy())
    return cands


def load_parent_sequence(max_n=MAX_N_SEARCH):
    """Load the provided SOTA sequence if it exists in globals."""
    obj = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
    if obj is None:
        return None
    try:
        arr = np.asarray(obj, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except Exception:
        return None

    if arr.size < 256:
        return None
    arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return None
    arr = arr / s

    if arr.size > max_n:
        target = 8192 if max_n >= 8192 else 4096
        blocks = int(arr.size) // int(target)
        if blocks > 1:
            use = int(target) * blocks
            arr = np.mean(arr[:use].reshape(int(target), blocks), axis=1)
        else:
            arr = arr[:target]
        s = float(arr.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
            arr = np.ones(target, dtype=np.float64) / float(target)
        else:
            arr = arr / s

    if arr.size > max_n:
        arr = arr[:max_n]
        s = float(arr.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
            arr = np.ones(|arr.size|, dtype=np.float64) / float(arr.size)
        else:
            arr = arr / s

    return arr


def best_structured_start(n):
    """Choose one of several simple analytic starts at length n by FFT score."""
    n = int(max(512, min(MAX_N_SEARCH, n)))
    best_p = None
    best_score = float("inf")

    options = []
    for alpha in (0.45, 0.50, 0.55):
        for smooth in (1.0, 2.0):
            options.append(make_power_profile(n, alpha, smooth, 0.0))
            options.append(make_power_profile(n, alpha, smooth, 0.1))
    for lam in (0.7, 1.4, 2.5):
        options.append(make_exponential_profile(n, lam))
    for lw in (0.75, 0.90):
        options.append(make_mixed_profile(n, lw))

    for p in options:
        sc = fft_score_only(p)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best_score = sc
            best_p = p.copy()

    if best_p is None:
        best_p = make_power_profile(n, 0.5, 1.0, 0.0)
        best_score = fft_score_only(best_p)
    return best_p, best_score


def optimize_sequence(p_start, time_budget, seed=0):
    """
    Local NTT/FFT log-probability descent for one length and one starting profile.
    Returns (best_p, best_fft_score, local_candidates).
    """
    start = time.monotonic()
    total = max(0.0, float(time_budget))
    if total <= 1.0:
        p = normalize_nonneg(p_start)
        sc = fft_score_only(p)
        return p, sc, [(sc, p)]

    p = normalize_nonneg(p_start)
    n = p.size
    if n < 512:
        p = make_power_profile(2048)
        n = p.size
    elif n > MAX_N_SEARCH:
        target = 8192
        blocks = int(n) // target
        if blocks > 1:
            use = target * blocks
            p = np.mean(p[:use].reshape(target, blocks), axis=1)
        else:
            p = p[:target]
        p = normalize_nonneg(p)
        n = p.size

    N = next_fast_len(2 * n - 1)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    phase_b = [20.0, 80.0, 250.0, 800.0, 2500.0]
    phase_f = [0.10, 0.20, 0.30, 0.25, 0.15]

    v = log_weights_from_p(p)
    beta = phase_b[0]
    A, B, Dmax, soft, w, cur_score = evaluate_fft(p, N, n, beta)
    if not np.isfinite(cur_score):
        p = make_power_profile(n)
        v = log_weights_from_p(p)
        A, B, Dmax, soft, w, cur_score = evaluate_fft(p, N, n, beta)

    best_p = p.copy()
    best_score = float(cur_score)
    best_v = v.copy()
    local = [(best_score, best_p)]

    momentum = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    lr = base_learning_rate(beta)
    stall = 0
    iter_no = 0

    phase_idx = 0
    cum_f = phase_f[0]
    phase_deadline = start + total * cum_f
    deadline = start + max(1.0, total - 1.0)

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        if phase_idx < len(phase_b) - 1 and time.monotonic() >= phase_deadline:
            phase_idx += 1
            cum_f += phase_f[phase_idx]
            phase_deadline = start + total * cum_f
            beta = phase_b[phase_idx]
            lr = base_learning_rate(beta)
            momentum *= 0.3

        if not np.isfinite(cur_score):
            p = best_p.copy()
            v = best_v.copy()
            A, B, Dmax, soft, w, cur_score = evaluate_fft(p, N, n, beta)
            momentum = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
            stall = 0
            continue

        # If the current point moved too far worse than local best, recenter.
        if cur_score > best_score + 0.05:
            p = best_p.copy()
            v = best_v.copy()
            A, B, Dmax, soft, w, cur_score = evaluate_fft(p, N, n, beta)
            momentum = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
            stall = 0
            continue

        corr = correlation_phi(A, w, N, n)
        d = make_direction(p, corr)
        if float(np.dot(d, d)) < 1e-18:
            d = normalize_direction(rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, size=n), clip=3.0)
        else:
            momentum = 0.70 * momentum + 0.30 * d
            dm = normalize_direction(momentum, clip=5.0)
            if float(np.dot(dm, dm)) > 1e-18:
                d = dm

        accepted = False
        accepted_attempt = -1
        lr_try = lr

        for attempt in range(5):
            v_try = v - lr_try * d
            v_try = np.where(np.isfinite(v_try), v_try, 0.0)
            v_try -= float(np.max(v_try))
            v_try = np.clip(v_try, -700.0, 0.0)

            p_try = safe_softmax(v_try)
            A_try, B_try, Dmax_try, soft_try, w_try, score_try = evaluate_fft(p_try, N, n, beta)

            if np.isfinite(score_try):
                if score_try < best_score - 1e-9:
                    best_score = float(score_try)
                    best_p = p_try.copy()
                    best_v = v_try.copy()
                    add_candidate_list(local, best_score, best_p, 8)

                if score_try < cur_score - 1e-10:
                    accepted = True
                    accepted_attempt = attempt
                    p = p_try
                    v = v_try
                    A = A_try
                    B = B_try
                    Dmax = Dmax_try
                    soft = soft_try
                    w = w_try
                    cur_score = score_try
                    break

                if soft_try < soft - 1e-10 and score_try <= cur_score + 0.005:
                    accepted = True
                    accepted_attempt = attempt
                    p = p_try
                    v = v_try
                    A = A_try
                    B = B_try
                    Dmax = Dmax_try
                    soft = soft_try
                    w = w_try
                    cur_score = score_try
                    break

            lr_try *= 0.5

        if accepted:
            stall = 0
            if accepted_attempt == 0:
                lr = min(0.35, lr * 1.03)
            else:
                lr = max(0.02, lr * 0.85)
        else:
            stall += 1
            lr = max(0.005, lr * 0.70)

            if stall > 80:
                sigma = 0.10 + 0.10 * (min(stall, 200) - 80) / 120.0
                v_try = best_v + rng.normal(0.0, sigma, size=n)
                v_try = np.where(np.isfinite(v_try), v_try, 0.0)
                v_try -= float(np.max(v_try))
                v_try = np.clip(v_try, -700.0, 0.0)
                p_try = safe_softmax(v_try)
                A_try, B_try, Dmax_try, soft_try, w_try, score_try = evaluate_fft(p_try, N, n, beta)

                if np.isfinite(score_try) and (score_try < best_score + 0.10 or rng.random() < 0.2):
                    p = p_try
                    v = v_try
                    A = A_try
                    B = B_try
                    Dmax = Dmax_try
                    soft = soft_try
                    w = w_try
                    cur_score = score_try
                else:
                    p = best_p.copy()
                    v = best_v.copy()
                    A, B, Dmax, soft, w, cur_score = evaluate_fft(p, N, n, beta)

                momentum = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
                stall = 0
                lr = base_learning_rate(beta)

        iter_no += 1
        if iter_no % 1000 == 0:
            print("optimize n=%d iter=%d cur=%.6f best=%.6f beta=%.0f" %
                  (n, iter_no, cur_score, best_score, beta))

    add_candidate_list(local, best_score, best_p, 8)
    return best_p, best_score, local


def exact_score_normalized(p):
    """Exact evaluator-compatible score using np.convolve on sum=1 input."""
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if p.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
    p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return float("inf")
    p = p / s
    n = p.size
    b = np.convolve(p, p, mode="full")
    if b.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    mx = float(np.max(b))
    if not np.isfinite(mx):
        return float("inf")
    return float(2.0 * n * mx)


def candidate_sort_key(item):
    """Sort key for candidate tuple (score, array)."""
    return item[0]


def refine_candidates(cands, time_limit):
    """Exact-check the best FFT candidates within a time limit."""
    start = time.monotonic()
    if cands is None or len(cands) == 0 or time_limit <= 0.0:
        return None, float("inf")

    ordered = sorted(cands, key=candidate_sort_key)
    best_exact = float("inf")
    best_arr = None

    for sc_fft, arr in ordered:
        used = time.monotonic() - start
        if used > max(0.0, time_limit * 0.85):
            break

        n = arr.size
        if n == 0 or n > MAX_EXACT_N:
            continue

        if np.isfinite(best_exact) and sc_fft > best_exact + 5e-4:
            break

        t0 = time.monotonic()
        sc_exact = exact_score_normalized(arr)
        dt = time.monotonic() - t0

        print("exact-check n=%d fft=%s exact=%s time=%.2f" %
              (n, ("%.6f" % sc_fft) if np.isfinite(sc_fft) else "inf",
               ("%.6f" % sc_exact) if np.isfinite(sc_exact) else "inf", dt))

        if np.isfinite(sc_exact) and sc_exact < best_exact:
            best_exact = float(sc_exact)
            best_arr = np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64).copy()

        if np.isfinite(best_exact) and best_exact < 1.40:
            break

    return best_arr, best_exact


def lbfgs_objective(v, grad):
    """
    Objective and gradient for scipy L-BFGS-B polish.
    State is held in global _LBF_STATE.  The objective is a scaled smooth maximum:
        value = scale*logsumexp(beta*D)
    where D=n*conv(p,p).
    """
    st = _LBF_STATE
    if st is None:
        if grad is None:
            return 1e100
        return 1e100, np.array([0.0])

    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(v)):
        g = np.zeros_like(v) if grad else None
        if grad:
            return 1e100, g
        return 1e100

    n = int(st["n"])
    N = int(st["N"])
    beta = float(st["beta"])
    scale = float(st["scale"])

    p = safe_softmax(v)
    A = np.fft.rfft(p, N)
    B = np.fft.irfft(A * A, N)[: 2 * n - 1]
    B = np.where(np.isfinite(B), B, 0.0)
    B = np.maximum(B, 0.0)

    if B.size == 0:
        val = 1e100
        if grad:
            return val, np.zeros_like(v)
        return val

    Dmax = float(n) * float(np.max(B))
    if not np.isfinite(Dmax):
        val = 1e100
        if grad:
            return val, np.zeros_like(v)
        return val

    mu = Dmax / float(n)
    x = beta * float(n) * (B - mu)
    x = np.clip(x, -745.0, 0.0)
    e = np.exp(x)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        val = 1e100
        if grad:
            return val, np.zeros_like(v)
        return val

    soft = Dmax + math.log(s) / beta
    val = scale * soft

    if not grad:
        return float(val)

    w = e / s
    corr = correlation_phi(A, w, N, n)
    dot = float(np.dot(p, corr))
    # derivative of scaled logsumexp(beta*D) wrt log-probability v
    g = (2.0 * float(n) * scale) * p * (corr - dot)
    g = np.where(np.isfinite(g), g, 0.0)
    return float(val), g


def polish_with_lbfgs(p, beta=300.0, maxiter=60):
    """
    Polish a candidate using L-BFGS-B on a scaled smooth max loss.
    If scipy is unavailable or polish worsens the candidate, return original.
    """
    if _SCIPY_OPT is None:
        return p, fft_score_only(p)

    p0 = normalize_nonneg(p)
    n = p0.size
    N = next_fast_len(2 * n - 1)
    v0 = log_weights_from_p(p0)

    global _LBF_STATE
    _LBF_STATE = {"N": N, "n": n, "beta": float(beta), "scale": 100.0}

    try:
        init_val = lbfgs_objective(v0, False)
        if not np.isfinite(init_val):
            return p0, fft_score_only(p0)

        res = _SCIPY_OPT.minimize(
            lbfgs_objective,
            v0,
            jac=True,
            method="L-BFGS-B",
            options={
                "maxiter": int(maxiter),
                "maxls": 10,
                "maxcor": 10,
                "gtol": 1e-8,
                "ftol": 1e-12,
                "disp": False
            }
        )
    except Exception:
        res = None

    best_p = p0
    best_sc = fft_score_only(p0)

    if res is not None and getattr(res, "x", None) is not None:
        vx = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if vx.size == n and np.all(np.isfinite(vx)):
            pn = safe_softmax(vx)
            scn = fft_score_only(pn)
            if np.isfinite(scn) and scn < best_sc:
                best_p = pn
                best_sc = scn

    # A second sharpening pass if the first improved things.
    if best_p is not p0 and np.isfinite(best_sc):
        v2 = log_weights_from_p(best_p)
        _LBF_STATE = {"N": N, "n": n, "beta": min(1500.0, 2.0 * float(beta)), "scale": 100.0}
        try:
            res2 = _SCIPY_OPT.minimize(
                lbfgs_objective,
                v2,
                jac=True,
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                options={
                    "maxiter": int(max(30, maxiter // 2)),
                    "maxls": 8,
                    "maxcor": 10,
                    "gtol": 1e-8,
                    "ftol": 1e-12,
                    "disp": False
                }
            )
            if res2 is not None and getattr(res2, "x", None) is not None:
                vx = np.asarray(res2.x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
                if vx.size == n and np.all(np.isfinite(vx)):
                    pn2 = safe_softmax(vx)
                    scn2 = fft_score_only(pn2)
                    if np.isfinite(scn2) and scn2 < best_sc:
                        best_p = pn2
                        best_sc = scn2
        except Exception:
            pass

    return best_p, best_sc


def finalize_sequence(p):
    """
    Convert an optimized probability vector into a valid list of heights.
    Objectively scale-invariant; scale to sum ~ sqrt(2n) to be safely above
    evaluator's tiny-sum threshold.
    """
    arr = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if arr.size == 0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(arr)):
        arr = np.ones(64, dtype=np.float64)

    arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
    n = arr.size

    s = float(arr.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        arr = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        s = float(n)

    scale = float(math.sqrt(2.0 * float(n)))
    arr = arr * (scale / float(s))
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)

    s2 = float(arr.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s2) or s2 < 0.01:
        arr = arr * (0.01 / max(s2, 1e-300))
        arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)

    return [float(x) for x in arr]


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    """
    Entry point.  Runs time-budgeted hybrid searches and returns the best
    sequence found, finalized for the evaluator.
    """
    global _LBF_STATE

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    try:
        total = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        total = 2400.0
    total = min(max(0.0, total), 2395.0)

    _LBF_STATE = None

    if total < 10.0:
        p0 = load_parent_sequence(MAX_N_SEARCH)
        if p0 is None:
            p0 = make_power_profile(2048)
        return finalize_sequence(p0)

    print("proposing with total budget %.1f s" % total)

    # Reserve time for polishing/final exact checks.
    reserve = 100.0 if total > 600.0 else 30.0
    opt_budget = max(10.0, total - reserve - 5.0)

    candidates = []

    parent = load_parent_sequence(MAX_N_SEARCH)
    if parent is not None:
        scp = fft_score_only(parent)
        add_candidate_list(candidates, scp, parent, 48)
        print("parent n=%d fft score=%.6f" % (parent.size, scp))

    # Main structured starts.
    if total > 600.0:
        n_main = 8192
    else:
        n_main = 4096

    p_main, sc_main = best_structured_start(n_main)
    add_candidate_list(candidates, sc_main, p_main, 48)
    print("structured n=%d fft score=%.6f" % (n_main, sc_main))

    n_small = 2048 if n_main <= 4096 else 4096
    p_small, sc_small = best_structured_start(n_small)
    add_candidate_list(candidates, sc_small, p_small, 48)
    print("structured n=%d fft score=%.6f" % (n_small, sc_small))

    p_big = None
    if total > 1500.0:
        p_big, sc_big = best_structured_start(16384)
        add_candidate_list(candidates, sc_big, p_big, 48)
        print("structured n=16384 fft score=%.6f" % sc_big)

    job_specs = []
    if parent is not None:
        job_specs.append((parent, 0.28))
    job_specs.append((p_main, 0.36 if parent is not None else 0.45))
    job_specs.append((p_small, 0.16 if parent is not None else 0.15))
    if p_big is not None:
        job_specs.append((p_big, 0.20 if parent is not None else 0.40))

    total_frac = sum(f for _, f in job_specs)
    if total_frac <= 0.0:
        total_frac = 1.0

    job_idx = 0
    for p0, frac in job_specs:
        secs = opt_budget * (frac / total_frac)
        if secs < 20.0:
            continue

        rem = total - reserve - (time.monotonic() - t0)
        if rem < 20.0:
            break

        p_use = normalize_nonneg(p0)
        print("job %d n=%d allocated=%.1f s start_fft=%.6f" %
              (job_idx, p_use.size, secs, fft_score_only(p_use)))

        bp, bs, local = optimize_sequence(p_use, secs, seed=seed + 101 * job_idx)
        if bp is not None and np.isfinite(bs):
            add_candidate_list(candidates, bs, bp, 48)
        if local:
            for it in local:
                add_candidate_list(candidates, it[0], it[1], 48)

        print("job %d ended best=%.6f absolute_time=%.1f" %
              (job_idx, bs, time.monotonic() - t0))
        job_idx += 1

    # Optional L-BFGS polish on the best FFT candidates.
    rem = total - 20.0 - (time.monotonic() - t0)
    if rem > 60.0 and _SCIPY_OPT is not None and candidates:
        top = sorted(candidates, key=candidate_sort_key)
        for idx in range(min(4, len(top))):
            if time.monotonic() - t0 > total - 30.0:
                break
            sc, arr = top[idx]
            if arr.size > MAX_N_SEARCH:
                continue
            print("polishing candidate n=%d fft=%.6f" % (arr.size, sc))
            pp, sp = polish_with_lbfgs(arr, beta=250.0, maxiter=55)
            if np.isfinite(sp):
                add_candidate_list(candidates, sp, pp, 48)

    # Final direct exact checks.
    rem = total - 5.0 - (time.monotonic() - t0)
    exact_limit = min(80.0, max(0.0, rem))
    best_final, best_exact = refine_candidates(candidates, exact_limit)

    if best_final is None or not np.isfinite(best_exact):
        if candidates:
            best_item = sorted(candidates, key=candidate_sort_key)[0]
            best_final = best_item[1]
            print("no exact verified best; using FFT best %.6f" % best_item[0])
        else:
            best_final = make_power_profile(4096)
            print("fallback structured profile")

    print("finalizing sequence length=%d" % best_final.size)
    return finalize_sequence(best_final)
```
